Tests: `PruneReleasesTests.AFolderWithNoRowIsRetiredToo`, `PruneReleasesTests.AFolderAboveTheHighestCommittedReleaseSurvivesBecauseAPublishInProgressMayOwnIt`, `ReleaseRetentionTests.ANumberAboveTheHighestCommittedReleaseIsNeverRetiredBecauseAPublishInProgressMayOwnIt`, `FileReleaseDirectoryTests.RetiringLeavesAFolderAboveTheHighestCommittedNumberBecauseAPublishInProgressMayOwnIt`, `FileReleaseDirectoryTests.RetiringIgnoresFoldersThatAreNotReleaseNamesSoNothingElseIsMoved`.
- The rename runs under the publication lock, so no activation can switch to a folder that is being moved. The delete runs after the lock ends, so file work never blocks review writers. Enforced by `PostgresPublicationStore.PruneAsync` and `PruneReleases`. Tests: `PostgresPublicationStoreTests.PruneRetiresFoldersWhileHoldingThePublicationLockSoNoActivationCanSwitchToAFolderBeingMoved`, `PruneReleasesTests.ARetiredFolderIsDeletedAfterTheStoreLockEndsSoReviewWritersAreNotBlockedByFileWork`.
- A retired folder leaves `releases/` in one rename, so no partial folder looks like a complete release. A later prune deletes leftovers. A delete never touches the hard-linked files of newer releases. Enforced by `FileReleaseDirectory`. Tests: `FileReleaseDirectoryTests.RetiringMovesFoldersThePlanRetiresOutOfReleasesSoNoHalfDeletedFolderCanLookLikeARelease`, `FileReleaseDirectoryTests.DeletingRetiredRemovesEveryTombstoneIncludingOnesAnEarlierCrashLeft`, `FileReleaseDirectoryTests.DeletingARetiredReleaseKeepsTheHardLinkedFilesOfNewerReleases`.
- A prune never fails a publish, also on shutdown. Every publish runs it. Enforced by `PruneReleases.ExecuteAsync` and `PublishRelease`. Tests: `PruneReleasesTests.AFailedDeleteIsLoggedNotThrownSoPruningNeverBlocksAPublish`, `PruneReleasesTests.ShutdownAfterTheCommitDoesNotFailThePublishAndStillRetiresUnderTheLock`, `PruneReleasesTests.APublishSucceedsWhenThePruneFailsBecauseOldFilesAreOnlyDiskUse`, `PruneReleasesTests.EveryPublishPrunesSoTheOldestFolderIsGoneOncePastTheKeepLimit`.
- A change of the base release after the build fails the commit, so the copied list is never stale. Enforced by `PostgresPublicationStore.RequireUnchangedReleasesAsync`. Test: `PostgresPublicationStoreTests.ReleaseBuiltBeforeARollbackIsRejectedSoItCannotRestoreWithdrawnRecords`.
- A carried record keeps its entries when a later publish renders its page again. Enforced by `ReleasePages` and the record file. Test: `PublishCorrectionsTests.ACarriedRecordKeepsItsEntriesWhenALaterPublishRendersItsPageAgain`.
- The owner CLI can publish with no record and no withdrawal. Then a change to an official profile, such as a photo takedown, or a changed bill status line reaches the public. It needs an active release. The publish is refused when no official profile and no bill status differs from the active release, unless the request has `--rewrite-officials`, `--rewrite-entries`, or `--rewrite-pages`. The message is "Nothing to publish: no record, official, or bill status changed." The refusal makes no release and discards the staged directory. The worker publish still needs at least one record, except for a status-only request. `PublishRelease` refuses any other empty request from the code subject. Enforced by `PublishRelease.ExecuteAsync`, `PublishRelease.StageAsync`, `ReleaseOfficialPages.ChangesProfiles`, `ReleaseBillStatuses.ChangedActionIds`, `PublishReleaseRequest.StatusOnly`, and `ReleaseCommand.ParsePublish`. Tests: `PublishApprovedRecordsTests.TheCodeSubjectCannotPublishAnEmptyRequestThatIsNotAStatusOnlyRequest`, `ReleaseCommandTests.TheRewriteOfficialsOptionPublishesWithNoRecordAndWritesTheOfficialPageAgain`, `ReleaseCommandTests.PublishWithOnlyAKeyAfterAWithholdFlagMakesAReleaseWithNoPhotoSoATakedownNeedsNoNewRecord`, `PublishOfficialPhotosTests.APublishWithNoRecordAfterAWithholdFlagMakesAReleaseWithNoPhotoSoATakedownNeedsNoNewRecord`, `PublishOfficialPhotosTests.APublishWithNoRecordAndNoChangedProfileIsRefusedAndLeavesNoReleaseOrStagedDirectory`, `PublishOfficialPhotosTests.APublishWithNoRecordAndNoActiveReleaseIsRefusedBecauseThereIsNothingToCarryForward`, `PublishOfficialPhotosTests.AChangedProfileAloneIsEnoughToPublishWithNoRecord`, `PublishApprovedRecordsTests.TheCodeSubjectCannotPublishAnEmptyRequestBecauseTheWorkerPublishesEveryKindOfRecord`.
- A withdrawal needs a note from a person. The publish refuses a blank, NUL, or over-long note. It refuses a withdrawal from the code subject. It refuses a record outside the active release, a record named twice, and a record that the request also publishes. Enforced by `PublicationWithdrawals`, `RecordNote.ValidText`, and `PublishRelease.ValidateRequest`. Tests: `PublishWithdrawalsTests.ABlankOrNulWithdrawalNoteIsRefusedBecauseAReaderMustSeeWhy`, `PublishWithdrawalsTests.AWithdrawalNoteOverTheContractLengthIsRefusedAndTheLimitLengthIsAccepted`, `PublishWithdrawalsTests.AWithdrawalNoteIsTrimmedAndKeepsOneCharacterForAWindowsLineBreakLikeACorrectionNote`, `PublishWithdrawalsTests.TheCodeSubjectCannotWithdrawBecauseOnlyAPersonJudgesThatARecordShouldGo`, `PublishWithdrawalsTests.ARecordOutsideTheActiveReleaseCannotBeWithdrawn`, `PublishWithdrawalsTests.ARecordThatIsAlreadyWithdrawnCannotBeWithdrawnAgain`, `PublishWithdrawalsTests.ARecordCannotBeBothPublishedAndWithdrawnInOneRequest`, `PublishWithdrawalsTests.TwoWithdrawalsOfOneRecordAndANullWithdrawalListAreRefused`.
- The withdrawals are part of the idempotency payload. Enforced by `ReviewValidation.HashPayload` over `PublishReleaseRequest`. Test: `PublishWithdrawalsTests.TheWithdrawalsAreInThePayloadHashSoTheSameKeyWithAnotherNoteIsAMismatchAndTheSameRequestReplays`.
- A withdrawal writes a notice and no record file. The manifest names the withdrawn revision, the dates, the note, and the correction history. Enforced by `PublicationWithdrawals.Create` and `PublishRelease`. Tests: `PublishWithdrawalsTests.AWithdrawalTakesTheRecordOutAndWritesANoticeWithTheRevisionTheDatesTheNoteAndTheHistory`, `PublishWithdrawalsTests.TheCommitNamesTheWithdrawnRecordSoTheStoreCanCheckThatReviewBlockedIt`.
- Each publish renders every withdrawal notice again, so a change to the page head reaches old notices. It never brings the withdrawn file back. Enforced by `PublishRelease.WriteWithdrawalPagesAsync`. Test: `PublishWithdrawalsTests.ALaterPublishWritesTheNoticeAgainSoItHasTheCurrentHeadAndNeverBringsTheWithdrawnFileBack`.
- The manifest lists withdrawals newest first, then by record ID. A record ID is unique across records and withdrawals. Records and withdrawals share the cap of 40,000. Enforced by `PublishRelease.StageAsync`, `PublicationRelease.Validate`, and `PublishedWithdrawal.Validate`. Tests: `PublishWithdrawalsTests.TheManifestListsTheNewestWithdrawalFirstAndBreaksATieByRecordId`, `PublicationReleaseTests.ARecordIdIsUniqueAcrossRecordsAndWithdrawalsBecauseOneNoticePageUsesTheRecordPagePath`, `PublicationReleaseTests.RecordsAndWithdrawalsShareOneCap`, `PublicationReleaseTests.AManifestWithAnInvalidOrMissingWithdrawalListIsRefused`, `PublishedWithdrawalTests`.
- Only a record that review blocked leaves the release. Enforced by `PostgresPublicationStore.RequireUnchangedReviewStateAsync` and `PublishedRecordRules.IsWithdrawable`. Tests: `PostgresPublicationStoreTests.OnlyARecordThatReviewBlockedCanLeaveTheReleaseForEveryKind`, `PostgresPublicationStoreTests.AWithdrawalOfARecordWithNoReviewRowIsRefused`.
- A record that a relationship or a brief of the new release cites cannot leave. A record that the same request adds cannot cite a withdrawn record. Enforced by `WithdrawalCascade` and `PublicationBuild.WithRemoved`. Tests: `PublishWithdrawalsTests.ARelationshipThatStaysInTheReleaseBlocksTheWithdrawalOfACitedRecordAndNamesTheCiter`, `PublishWithdrawalsTests.WithdrawingTheCitingRelationshipInTheSameRequestPassesAndEachCitedPageDropsTheLink`, `PublishWithdrawalsTests.ABriefThatStaysInTheReleaseBlocksTheWithdrawalOfACitedRecordUnlessTheBriefLeavesToo`, `PublishWithdrawalsTests.ANewRelationshipThatCitesARecordWithdrawnInTheSameRequestIsRefused`.
- A withdrawn relationship leaves the related panel of each record that it cited. Another link of the same record stays. Enforced by `ReleaseLinks` and `ReleasePages`. Tests: `PublishWithdrawalsTests.AWithdrawnRelationshipRemovesItsLinkFromThePagesOfBothRecordsItCited`, `PublishWithdrawalsTests.AnotherLinkOfACitedRecordStaysOnItsPageWhenOneRelationshipIsWithdrawn`.
- A withdrawn record leaves the official pages and the vote files of its officials. A Congress with no row left disappears. Enforced by `ReleaseOfficialPages` and `ReleaseOfficialVotes`. Tests: `PublishWithdrawalsTests.AWithdrawnStatementLeavesTheOfficialPageAndAnOfficialWithNoRecordLeftLosesThePage`, `PublishWithdrawalsTests.AWithdrawnVoteLeavesTheVoteFilesOfItsMembersAndACongressWithNoRowDisappears`.
- No carried page links a withdrawn action. A vote page on the bill of a withdrawn action is written again without the bill link. Enforced by `ReleasePages` and `VoteRecordLoader`. Tests: `PublishWithdrawalsTests.AVotePageThatLinkedTheWithdrawnActionOfItsBillIsWrittenAgainWithoutTheLink`, `PostgresHouseVoteTests.FindBillActionRecordIdsOfVotesUsesTheBillOfThePublishedRevisionAndNotTheBillOfTheCurrentCandidate`, `PostgresHouseVoteTests.FindBillActionRecordIdsOfVotesReadsTheNamedRevisionNumberAndNotAnotherStoredRevisionOfTheRecord`.
- The publish refuses a null withdrawal and a default list. Enforced by `PublishRelease.ValidateRequest`. Test: `PublishWithdrawalsTests.ANullWithdrawalIsRefusedWithAnArgumentException`.
- A withdrawal requeues the brief of the week that first published the record. Enforced by `BriefStaleness.RequeueAfterPublicationAsync`. Test: `BriefStalenessTests.AWithdrawalRequeuesTheBriefOfTheWeekThatFirstPublishedTheWithdrawnRecord`.
- A person returns a withdrawn record only with a note, at a revision above the withdrawn revision. The correction list continues from the withdrawal, and one entry is added for the withdrawn revision with the return note. The cap of 20 notes applies. Enforced by `PublicationCorrections`, `PublicationCorrections.LoadAsync`, and `PublishedRecordLoader.RequirePublishable`. Tests: `PublishWithdrawalsTests.APersonBringsAWithdrawnRecordBackWithANoteAndItsHistoryKeepsTheEarlierCorrectionsAndTheReturn`, `PublishWithdrawalsTests.AReturnWithoutANoteIsRefusedBecauseAReaderMustSeeWhyTheRecordCameBack`, `PublishWithdrawalsTests.AReturnAtTheWithdrawnRevisionIsRefusedBecauseReviewNeverChangedTheRecordThatItBlocked`, `PublishWithdrawalsTests.AReturnKeepsTheNoteCapOfTheCorrectionListSoALongHistoryCannotGrowWithoutLimit`.
- The worker returns a withdrawn record only when a person approved a higher revision. A code approval leaves the record withdrawn on every path, also `releases publish`. A revision that is not higher leaves it withdrawn too. The stage `publishing` logs one line, leaves the record out, and reports no failure. A person approval without a note raises `MissingCorrectionNoteException`, which the stage also logs and skips. The stage passes the skipped IDs to the store lists of approved records, and SQL leaves them out. The page size stays at the limit, so a withdrawn backlog cannot starve other records. Enforced by `PublishedRecordLoader.RequirePublishable`, `PublicationCorrections.For`, `PublishApprovedRecords`, and `ReviewRecords.ReadUnpublishedApprovedAsync`. Tests: `PublishWithdrawalsTests.TheCodeSubjectCannotBringAWithdrawnRecordBackWithACodeApprovedRevisionBecauseOnlyAPersonJudgesThatItMayReturn`, `PublishWithdrawalsTests.TheOwnerCannotBringAWithdrawnRecordBackWithACodeApprovedRevisionEither`, `PublishWithdrawalsTests.TheWorkerReturnsAWithdrawnRecordWhenAPersonApprovedAHigherRevisionWithANoteAndKeepsTheHistory`, `PublishWithdrawalsTests.TheWorkerKeepsAWithdrawnRecordOffTheSiteWhenThePersonApprovalHasNoNote`, `PublishWithdrawalsTests.TheCodeSubjectIsRefusedBeforeTheRevisionCheckSoAWithdrawnFactAtItsWithdrawnRevisionStaysQuietForTheStage`, `PublishApprovedRecordsTests.ATickReturnsAWithdrawnRecordThatAPersonApprovedAgainWithANoteAndLeavesOneWithoutANoteWhileTheOthersPublish`, `PublishApprovedRecordsTests.AWithdrawnRecordIsLeftOutWithALogAndNoFailureSoATickNeverPublishesItAgainAndNobodyIsPaged`, `PublishApprovedRecordsTests.AWithdrawnBacklogLargerThanTheBatchLimitDoesNotStarveTheOtherFactRecords`, `PostgresBillStreamTests.TheReadyListLeavesOutTheExcludedIdsInSqlSoSkippedRecordsNeverFillItsLimit`.
- A returned record leaves `withdrawals`, goes back to `records`, and keeps its first publication time. The withdrawal note and date stay only in earlier release directories. The page of the record replaces the notice. Enforced by `PublishRelease`, `PublicationBuild.FirstPublishedAt`, and the seven record builders. Tests: `PublishWithdrawalsTests.AReturnedRecordKeepsItsFirstPublishedDateSoReadersSeeWhenTheRecordFirstWentPublic`, `PublishWithdrawalsTests.APersonBringsAWithdrawnRecordBackWithANoteAndItsHistoryKeepsTheEarlierCorrectionsAndTheReturn`.
- A returned vote brings back the rows of its members in the vote files. A returned action writes again the carried vote pages on its bill, so they link it again. A relationship of the same request may cite the returned record. Enforced by `ReleaseOfficialVotes`, `ReleasePages`, and `PublicationBuild.WithAdded`. Tests: `PublishWithdrawalsTests.AReturnedVoteBringsBackTheVoteRowsOfItsMembers`, `PublishWithdrawalsTests.AReturnedActionWritesAgainTheCarriedVotePagesOfItsBillSoTheyLinkItAgain`, `PublishWithdrawalsTests.ARelationshipInTheSameRequestMayCiteTheReturnedRecord`.
- A withdrawn record returns only when review approved a revision above the withdrawn revision, and the approval holds a correction note. Enforced by `PublishedRecordRules.CanReturn`, which `PublishedRecordLoader.RequirePublishable` applies, and by `PublicationCorrections`, which refuses a return without a note with `MissingCorrectionNoteException`. Test: `PublishWithdrawalsTests.AReturnWithoutANoteIsRefusedBecauseAReaderMustSeeWhyTheRecordCameBack`.
- The blocked list and the withdrawal commit use one rule and one read of the review status. Enforced by `PublishedRecordRules.IsWithdrawable` and `PostgresPublicationStore.ReadReviewStatusesAsync`. Tests: `PostgresPublicationStoreTests.TheBlockedListAndTheWithdrawalCommitAgreeOnEveryReviewStatus`, `PostgresPublicationStoreTests.TheStatusReadReportsTheCurrentReviewStatusForEveryKindAndSkipsARecordWithNoReviewRow`.
- The blocked list holds the records of the active manifest whose review status is blocking. A withdrawn notice is not listed for its review status alone. The list also holds a record with a person-approved revision that the release does not hold, when the record has 20 corrections with a note. The worker skips such a record at every tick. A code-approved revision at the cap is not listed, because it can publish. The list also holds a withdrawn notice with 20 corrections with a note when a person approved a higher revision, because that record cannot return. The list is empty with no active release. Enforced by `ListBlockedPublished` and `BlockedPublishedRecords`. Tests: `ListBlockedPublishedTests.ARecordThatReviewBlockedIsListedWithWhatTheOwnerNeedsToWithdrawIt`, `ListBlockedPublishedTests.APersonRevisionStuckAtTheCorrectionCapIsListedWithTheReasonBecauseTheWorkerSkipsItForever`, `ListBlockedPublishedTests.ARecordBelowTheCorrectionCapIsNotListedBecauseAPersonRevisionStillPublishes`, `ListBlockedPublishedTests.ACodeApprovedRevisionAtTheCorrectionCapIsNotListedBecauseCodeUpdatesAlwaysPublish`, `ListBlockedPublishedTests.AWithdrawnRecordAtTheCapWithAPersonRevisionIsListedBecauseItCannotReturn`, `ListBlockedPublishedTests.ARollbackToAReleaseWithAWithdrawnRecordAtTheCapReportsItBecauseItStillCannotReturn`, `ListBlockedPublishedTests.AWithdrawnRecordIsNotListedBelowTheCapOrForACodeRevisionBecauseOnlyAPersonRevisionAtTheCapIsStuck`, `ListBlockedPublishedTests.OneStatusReadAndOneRecordFileReadForTheCandidateKeepTheListBounded`, `ListBlockedPublishedTests.ARecordThatReviewStillAllowsIsNotListedBecauseWithdrawingItWouldBeRefused`, `ListBlockedPublishedTests.ANoticeBlockedOnlyByReviewStatusIsNotListedBecauseTheRecordAlreadyLeftTheRelease`, `ListBlockedPublishedTests.NoActiveReleaseGivesAnEmptyList`.
- The publisher and the blocked list read the correction cap from one rule, `PublishedCorrection.IsAtNoteLimit`. Enforced by `PublicationCorrections` and `BlockedPublishedRecords`. Test: `ListBlockedPublishedTests.ThePublisherRefusesAPersonRevisionAtTheCapAndTheListHoldsTheSameRecordFromOneState`.
- The store names the actor of the newest approval of a record, for each of the seven kinds. It does so only for an approved revision that the release does not hold. Enforced by `PostgresPublicationStore.ReadReviewStatusesAsync`. Tests: `PostgresPublicationStoreTests.TheStatusReadNamesTheApprovalActorFromTheDecisionTableOfEveryKind`, `PostgresPublicationStoreTests.TheStatusReadNamesTheNewestApprovalSoACodeApprovalAfterAPersonApprovalGivesTheCodeActor`, `PostgresPublicationStoreTests.TheStatusReadNamesTheActorOfTheLatestApprovalOnlyForAnApprovedRevisionThatTheReleaseDoesNotHold`.
- A stuck row shows the badge "Cannot publish" and its reason text, and never the withdraw command, because a withdrawal does not free it. For an action, a vote, a statement, and an executive action, the text says that only a code-approved revision can publish. Code approves those kinds. For a document change, a brief, or a relationship, the text says that no new revision can publish. For a withdrawn record, it says that the record cannot return. Enforced by `BlockedPublishedRecord.StuckText` and `ReviewHome`. Tests: `ListBlockedPublishedTests.TheReasonTextOffersCodeApprovalOnlyForAKindThatCodeCanApprove`, `ListBlockedPublishedTests.AWithdrawnRecordAtTheCapWithAPersonRevisionIsListedBecauseItCannotReturn`, `ReviewWorkspaceTests.ARecordStuckAtTheCorrectionCapShowsOnceInTheQueueAsCannotPublishWithTheReasonAsync`. The CLI prints the reason of each row. Test: `ReleaseCommandTests.BlockedNamesTheReasonOfEachRowSoTheOwnerSeesAStuckRecordIsNotOneToWithdraw`.
- Only the owner lists blocked records, in the CLI and in the stuck rows of the queue. Enforced by `ListBlockedPublished` and `ReviewHome`. Test: `ListBlockedPublishedTests.OnlyTheOwnerMayListBlockedRecordsBecauseOnlyTheOwnerMayWithdraw`.
- The activate command reports the restored withdrawals, the records withdrawn again, and the blocked records. It compares with the release that the link serves. It does not fail after the switch if a read fails, and each failed read empties only its own lists. Enforced by `ActivatePublicationRelease`. Tests: `PublicationReleaseHandlersTests.RollingBackBeforeAWithdrawalReportsTheRestoredRecordBecauseReadersSeeItAgain`, `PublicationReleaseHandlersTests.ActivatingAReleaseThatWithdrewARecordReportsItWithdrawnAgain`, `PublicationReleaseHandlersTests.RollingBackToAReleaseWithARecordThatReviewNowBlocksReportsItBecauseReadersSeeItAgain`, `PublicationReleaseHandlersTests.ACleanRollbackReportsNothing`, `PublicationReleaseHandlersTests.AManifestThatCannotBeReadAfterTheSwitchStillReturnsTheSummaryAndLogsAWarning`, `PublicationReleaseHandlersTests.AnUnreadableReplacedManifestEmptiesOnlyTheComparisonListsAndKeepsTheBlockedList`, `PublicationReleaseHandlersTests.TheListsDescribeTheReleaseThatIsServedWhenAnotherActivationWonTheRace`.
- The command `releases blocked` prints the list, and `releases activate` prints the summary and the three lists in one object. Enforced by `ReleaseCommand`. Tests: `ReleaseCommandTests.BlockedListsThePublishedRecordsThatReviewBlockedSoTheOwnerKnowsWhatToWithdraw`, `ReleaseCommandTests.ActivateNestsTheSummaryNextToTheRollbackWarningsSoOneObjectCarriesBoth`.
- A request cannot return and withdraw one record. Enforced by `PublishRelease.ValidateRequest`. Test: `PublishWithdrawalsTests.AReturnAndAWithdrawalOfOneRecordInOneRequestAreRefused`.
- A review change between the build and the commit of a return fails the commit. Enforced by `PostgresPublicationStore.RequireUnchangedReviewStateAsync`. Test: `PostgresPublicationStoreTests.AReviewChangeBetweenTheBuildAndTheCommitOfAReturnFailsTheCommit`.
- The notice page shows the kind, the record ID, the withdrawn revision, the two dates, the note, and the corrections. It shows no headline, body, or officials. Enforced by `PublishedWithdrawalPage` and `PublishedWithdrawalView`. Test: `ReleaseRendererTests.AWithdrawalNoticeShowsTheKindTheRevisionTheDatesTheEscapedNoteAndTheHistoryButNoRecordContent`.
- The option `--withdraw` needs a record and a note, and bad input exits with code 2. Enforced by `ReleaseCommand`. Tests: `ReleaseCommandTests.InvalidInputExitsWithTwoBeforeAnyHandlerRuns`, `ReleaseCommandTests.WithdrawOptionWithdrawsTheNamedRecordWithoutAnyRecordToPublishAndAnUnpublishedRecordExitsWithTwo`.
- The record page and the Timeline show both entry types. Enforced by `PublishedRecordFrame`, `PublishedCorrectionView`, and `PublishedTimelineView`. Tests: `ReleaseRendererTests.AReplacedRecordShowsEachCorrectionWithItsNoteOrTheUpdatedFromTheSourceLabelAndAddsItToTheTimeline`, `ReleaseRendererTests.AVoteWhoseTextAloneChangedSaysSoOnThePageAndInTheTimeline`, `ReleaseRendererTests.ARecordWithNoCorrectionsShowsNoCorrectionList`.
- An approval keeps a correction note that has 1 to 500 characters and no NUL, with each Windows line break counted as one character. Only an approval takes a note. Enforced by `ReviewValidation.CreateDecision` through `CorrectionNoteText.Validate`, which the withdrawal note check also uses. Tests: `DecideVoteReviewCorrectionNoteTests.AnApprovalKeepsTheTrimmedCorrectionNoteSoThePublishStepCanWriteItIntoTheRecordHistory`, `DecideVoteReviewCorrectionNoteTests.ABlankOrNulCorrectionNoteIsRefused`, `DecideVoteReviewCorrectionNoteTests.ACorrectionNoteOverTheContractLengthIsRefusedAndTheLimitLengthIsAccepted`, `DecideVoteReviewCorrectionNoteTests.OnlyAnApprovalTakesACorrectionNoteBecauseNothingElseReachesAReader`, and `ReviewWorkspaceTests.ApprovingAReplacementWithACorrectionNoteLetsItPublishWithThatNoteAsync`.
- A published correction holds the note of the approval that the release publishes, and a note on a first publication is dropped. Enforced by `PublicationCorrections.For` through `PublishedRecordRules.CorrectionNoteOf`. Tests: `PublishCorrectionsTests.APersonReplacementWithANoteWritesAnEntryThatNamesTheReplacedRevision`, `PublishCorrectionsTests.ANoteOnAFirstPublicationIsDroppedBecauseNothingWasCorrected`, `PublishCorrectionsTests.APersonPublishOverTheNotedCapFailsLoudInsteadOfDroppingTheOldestEntry`, and `ReleaseCommandTests.AReplacementPublishesWithTheNoteOfItsApprovalAndWithoutOneExitsWithTwo`.
- An issue or policy area page lists only the manifest entries that hold its ID. Enforced by `ReleaseFacets`. Test: `PublishIndexPagesTests.AnIssuePageListsOnlyTheRecordsThatHoldItsIdSoReadersDoNotSeeOtherTopics`.
- A facet has one page for each 50 entries. A facet ID that cannot be a path segment gets no page, and the publish still succeeds. Enforced by `ReleaseFacet.HasPages`, which calls `PublicationProtocol.IsPathSegment`. Tests: `PublishIndexPagesTests.AFacetPageSetStaysAtOnePagePerFiftyRecordsSoThePageCountIsBounded` and `PublishIndexPagesTests.AFacetIdThatCannotBeAPathSegmentGetsNoPageAndDoesNotFailThePublish`.
- The first page of an issue opens with the panel "Votes and bills tagged with this issue". The first page of a policy area opens with the panel "Bills tagged with this policy area". Code counts over all pages of the facet. The issue panel counts the roll call votes. It then counts the split votes, the votes where both parties agreed, and the votes in neither group. Each of those three lines shows only when its count is above zero. It then counts the bills and resolutions. The policy area panel counts only bills and resolutions, because a vote entry never holds a policy area. Every bill type that the collector reads can reach an action record, resolutions included. The issue lede says that a model picks the tag of a bill or vote and no person checks it. This is what `method.html` says, and the lede links to that page. The panel says that a tag does not say if a record supports or opposes the issue. The counts use only manifest fields, so the panel adds no read and no contract field. Enforced by `FacetSummary`, `PublishedFacetSummaryView`, and `PublishedFacetPage.razor`, which computes the summary once. `PublishedOfficialView` owns the meanings of a split vote and of a vote where both parties agreed. The badges and the panel use them. Tests: `FacetSummaryTests.ItCountsVotesAndBillsApartAndLeavesOtherKindsOutSoNoCountClaimsMoreThanTheTagsShow`, `ReleaseRendererTests.AnIssuePageSummaryCountsEveryPageOfItsFacetSoAVoteOnPageTwoStillCountsAndEachLineStatesWhatItCounts`, `ReleaseRendererTests.AnIssuePageSummaryOmitsTheSplitAgreedAndOtherLinesWhenTheirCountIsZeroAndOnlyTheFirstPageShowsThePanel`, and `ReleaseRendererTests.APolicyAreaPageSummaryCountsOnlyBillsBecauseAVoteEntryNeverCarriesAPolicyArea`.
- A facet page shows the configured name, or the ID when the catalog has no name for it. Enforced by `PublicationCatalog`. Test: `PublishIndexPagesTests.AnIssuePageShowsTheConfiguredNameAndAnUnconfiguredIdShowsItselfSoACarriedRecordNeverBlocksAPublish`.
- An issue page shows the panel "What this issue covers" on its first page. The panel holds the definition, the list "Includes", and the list "Does not include". An issue with no excludes shows no "Does not include" list. A policy area page shows no such panel. `config/issues.json` owns the text. `PublicationCatalog` reads it once, and `ReleaseFacets` gives it to `ReleaseFacet.Definition`, as it does for the icon. `catalog.json` and `PublishedCatalogEntry` do not hold it. Enforced by `PublicationCatalog`, `ReleaseFacets`, and `PublishedFacetPage`. Tests: `ReleaseRendererTests.AnIssuePageShowsItsDefinitionAndBothListsAndAPolicyAreaPageShowsNoneSoOnlyIssuesExplainThemselves` and `ReleaseRendererTests.AnIssueWithNoExcludesShowsNoDoesNotIncludeListSoNoEmptyListMisleadsTheReader`.
- A record page and the issue panel of an official show the configured name of each issue. The name links to the issue page. They show an unnamed issue ID as plain text with no link. The statement page, the relationship page, and the action page follow the same rule for policy areas, with a link to the policy area page. The action page keeps the Congress.gov name as the link text. Except on the action page, the name comes from `PublicationCatalog` at render time, and the published JSON keeps the IDs. A carried page keeps its old name after a rename. A later publish that renders the page again shows the new name. Enforced by `PublishedRecordView.IssueLink` and `PublishedRecordView.PolicyAreaLink`, which share one link-or-plain-text rule. Configuration validation (`IssueConfiguration.ValidateId`) limits a configured ID to a path segment, so each link has a page. Tests: `IssueConfigurationRevisionTests.MalformedIssueIdIsRejected`, `ReleaseRendererTests.ARecordPageShowsTheConfiguredIssueNameLinkedToItsIssuePageAndAnUnconfiguredIdAsPlainText`, `ReleaseRendererTests.AnActionStatementAndRelationshipPageShowTheConfiguredPolicyAreaNameLinkedToItsPageAndAnUnconfiguredIdAsPlainText`, `IssueConfigurationRevisionTests.MalformedPolicyAreaIdIsRejected`, `PublishedOfficialPageTests.TheIssueColumnShowsTheConfiguredNameLinkedToTheIssuePageAndAnUnconfiguredIdAsPlainText`.
- A link back to the source text or to a related record names its target. So no two links on a page have the same accessible name. The visible link text stays the same. A visually hidden suffix names the target, for example "Show this quote in the saved text: statement, quote 2". `PublishedRecordView.QuoteTarget` builds the suffix as "subject, quote N", or "subject, quote" when the subject has one quote. A "Source line" link on a fact row names its row, for example "Source line: Signed". `PublishedRecordView.SourceLineTarget` adds "line N" when a subject has several lines. Enforced by `ContextLink`, `SourceLineLink`, `PublishedRecordView.QuoteTarget`, and `PublishedRecordView.SourceLineTarget`. Test: `ReleaseRendererTests.EveryLinkBackToTheSourceTextOrTheRelationNamesItsTargetSoNoTwoLinksOnAPageSoundTheSame`.
- `<main>` has `tabindex="-1"`, so the skip link moves focus to it. Enforced by `PublicPage`. A click on plain text in `<main>` also moves focus to it. The browser does not match `:focus-visible` on mouse focus, so no ring shows. Focus from the skip link by keyboard shows the ring. Checked in Blink. Test: `ReleaseRendererTests.MainTakesFocusFromTheSkipLinkOnEveryPageType`.
- The report page has one live region for success (`role="status"`) and one for failure (`role="alert"`). `public.js` fills only the region that fits the outcome and focuses it. Focus stays on the alert region, so a repeat failure is read again. Some screen readers read it twice. Enforced by `PublishedReportPage` and `public.js`. Test for the markup: `ReleaseRendererTests.ReportPageAnnouncesAFailureThroughItsOwnAlertRegionSoNoReaderMissesAFailedSend`. No test for the script, because the repository has no JavaScript test harness.
- The border of a form control and of the workspace theme toggle uses `--control-line`, not `--line-strong`. The token is 3.4:1 on the white surface, 3.1:1 on the page ground, and 3.2:1 on the raised color in the light theme. It is 3.4:1 on the surface, 3.7:1 on the ground, and 3.05:1 on the raised color in the dark theme. WCAG 1.4.11 needs 3:1. `--line-strong` stays for rules and dots, which are not control borders. The review UI uses the same controls, so its borders change too. Enforced by `theme.css` and `app.css`. No test. Checked: the computed style in Blink on pages from the renderer test fixtures.
- The pressed button of a segmented switch shows a 2 pixel bar under its label. So does the current link of the review `FilterTabs`. The bar comes with a color change and `aria-pressed` or `aria-current`. Every segmented switch and `FilterTabs` use it. The cue does not rely on color. The bar uses the text color, so it shows in both themes. Enforced by `app.css`. No test. Checked: Blink screenshots of the switches and `FilterTabs` in both themes.
- Each publish rebuilds the facet pages and the home page, and the home page shows the latest brief, also when the publish only carries it. Enforced by `ReleaseIndexPages`. Tests: `PublishIndexPagesTests.EveryPublishRebuildsTheFacetPagesSoTheyListTheCarriedRecordsAndTheNewOne` and `PublishIndexPagesTests.HomeShowsTheBriefOfTheNewestWeekEvenWhenAnOlderWeekIsPublishedLater`.
- `officials.json` lists an official only when the release holds a published record that names the official. Enforced by `ReleaseDataFiles`, `PublicationOfficials`, and `PostgresVoteReviewStore.ReadMembersAsync`, which reads the member ids of the named revisions in SQL. It refuses a revision of another kind, a revision that is not stored, and a stored revision with no member. Tests: `PublishOfficialsTests.OnlyAnOfficialWithAPublishedRecordIsListedSoNobodyAppearsBeforeTheirFirstRecord`, `PublishOfficialsTests.AReleaseWithNoOfficialRecordListsNoOfficial`, `PublishOfficialsTests.ACosponsorIsNotListedBecauseOnlyTheSponsorOwnsTheAction`, `PublishOfficialsTests.AHouseVoteMemberIsListedEvenThoughTheVoteEntryNamesNoOfficial`, `PublishOfficialsTests.AVoteMemberStaysListedWhenALaterReleaseCarriesTheVote`, `PublishOfficialsTests.AReplacedVoteRevisionListsTheMembersOfTheNewRevisionNotTheCarriedOne`, `PostgresHouseVoteTests.ReadMembersReturnsTheRecordAndPersonOfEachMemberOfTheNamedRevisionsInOneQueryAndRefusesARevisionThatIsNotStored`, `PostgresHouseVoteTests.ReadMembersReadsAStoredRevisionAndRefusesTheCallWhenAnotherNamedRevisionIsNotStored`, `PostgresHouseVoteTests.ReadMembersListsAPersonOnceWhenTheRevisionNamesThePersonTwice`, `PostgresHouseVoteTests.ReadMembersFailsForAStoredRevisionWithoutMembersInsteadOfDroppingTheVote`.
- A person matches a roster term by the bioguide ID, else by the Wikidata ID, else has no terms. Enforced by `PublicationOfficials`. Tests: `PublishOfficialsTests.AMemberMatchedByBioguideGetsTheDatedTermsOfTheRoster`, `PublishOfficialsTests.AnExecutiveMatchedByWikidataGetsPresidentTermsWithNoStateOrDistrict`, `PublishOfficialsTests.AFormerMemberWhoIsNotInTheCurrentRosterFallsBackToTheExecutiveTermsByWikidata`, `PublishOfficialsTests.APersonWithoutRosterRowsIsListedByNameOnlyAndAWikidataIdWithoutASnapshotOfficialIsNotAnError`.
- A website starts with `https://`, has no white space, and is an absolute URL of at most 512 characters with no user info. The profile of `officials.json` holds a website only when it follows this rule, and the page link uses `rel="noopener noreferrer"`. Enforced by `PublishedWebsite`, `PublishedOfficialProfile.Validate`, `PublicationOfficials`, and `PublishedOfficialPage`. Tests: `PublishedOfficialsTests.AProfileWebsiteThatBreaksTheRuleIsRefusedSoTheReaderNeverLinksAnUnsafeUrl`, `PublishedOfficialsTests.AProfileWebsiteOverTheLengthCapIsRefused`, `PublishOfficialsTests.ARosterUrlThatBreaksTheWebsiteRuleBecomesNoWebsiteAndDoesNotFailThePublish`, `PublishOfficialsTests.AMemberGetsTheWebsiteOfTheRoster`, `PublishOfficialsTests.AnExecutiveGetsTheConfiguredWebsiteBecauseTheExecutiveRosterHasNoUrl`, `PublishOfficialsTests.AValidRosterUrlWinsOverTheConfiguredWebsiteBecauseTheRosterTracksTheCurrentOffice`, `PublishOfficialsTests.AFormerMemberWhoIsNotInTheCurrentRosterTakesTheConfiguredWebsite`, `PublishedOfficialPageTests.TheCurrentPageLinksTheOfficialWebsiteNamingItsHostWithNoReferrerAndNoOpener`.
- A change of the website rewrites the page of the official, because the publish compares the whole profile. Enforced by `ReleaseOfficialPages`. Test: `PublishOfficialPagesTests.AWebsiteChangeRewritesTheOfficialPageBecauseTheProfileChanged`.
- A photo path is `photos/`, 64 lowercase hex characters, and `.jpg`. The credit is bounded text. The source URL follows the website rule. The width and the height are from 1 to `PublishedPhoto.MaximumDimension`. Enforced by `PublishedPhoto`, `PublicationProtocol.IsPhotoPath`, and `PublishedOfficialProfile.Validate`. Tests: `PublishedOfficialsTests.APhotoPathThatIsNotAHashedFileInThePhotosFolderIsRefusedSoAProfileCannotNameAnotherFile`, `PublishedOfficialsTests.APhotoSourceUrlThatIsNotAPlainHttpsUrlIsRefused`, `PublishedOfficialsTests.APhotoSizeThatIsNotPositiveAndBoundedIsRefused`, `PublishedOfficialsTests.ABlankPhotoCreditIsRefusedSoEveryPhotoNamesItsSource`, `PublishedOfficialsTests.AnInvalidPhotoMakesTheProfileInvalid`, `PublishedOfficialsTests.AProfilePhotoKeepsItsFieldsThroughAJsonRoundTrip`, `PublishedOfficialsTests.AProfileWithNoPhotoIsValidSoAReleaseBeforePhotosStillReads`.
- The photo of a profile is the newest stored photo row of the person, whatever photo source the collection configuration names. A person with no row has no photo. One query reads the rows of the configured people, once for each publish. A later invariant in this list states the withhold rule. Enforced by `PostgresMemberRosterStore.ReadNewestPhotosAsync` and `PublishRelease`. Tests: `PostgresMemberPhotoStoreTests.TheNewestPhotoOfAPersonIsTheRowOfItsLatestAttemptEvenWhenAnEarlierBodyReturned`, `PostgresMemberPhotoStoreTests.OnlyTheNamedPeopleAreReadSoAnotherPersonOrAPersonWithNoRowAddsNothing`, `PublishOfficialPhotosTests.AnOfficialWithNoPhotoRowGetsNoPhotoSoNoFileIsWritten`, `PublishOfficialPhotosTests.APublishWritesTheCapturedImageUnderItsCaptureHashEvenWhenTheConfigurationHasNoPhotoSource`, `PublishOfficialPhotosTests.APublishReadsThePhotoRowsOnceSoNoSecondReadCanDisagreeWithTheFirst`.
- A new photo is written from its capture under its hash. Two officials with one capture share one file, which is written and read once, also when the read fails. Only the officials with a published record have their captures read. Enforced by `ReleasePhotos` and `ReleaseDataFiles`. Tests: `PublishOfficialPhotosTests.APublishWritesTheCapturedImageUnderItsCaptureHashEvenWhenTheConfigurationHasNoPhotoSource`, `PublishOfficialPhotosTests.TwoOfficialsWithOneCaptureShareOneFileSoTheFileIsWrittenAndReadOnce`, `PublishOfficialPhotosTests.TwoOfficialsWithOneUnreadableCaptureReadItOnceAndEachLogsAnError`, `PublishOfficialPhotosTests.APersonWithNoPublishedRecordHasNoCaptureReadSoAnUnlistedPhotoCostsNothingAtEachPublish`.
- A person with `withholdPhoto` in the collection configuration has no photo. `PublicationOfficials.ReadPhotosAsync` reads photo rows only for people without the flag. So the profile has no photo and no capture is read. `ReleasePhotos` links no file forward. The photo of the previous release cannot come back, also when a newer photo is unreadable. The changed profile rewrites the official page. Removing the flag restores the newest photo at the next publish. Enforced by `PublicationOfficials.ReadPhotosAsync`. Tests: `PublishOfficialPhotosTests.AWithheldPersonHasNoPhotoAndNoCaptureIsRead`, `PublishOfficialPhotosTests.APhotoThatThePreviousReleaseHeldIsNotLinkedForwardOnceWithheld`, `PublishOfficialPhotosTests.WithholdingRewritesTheOfficialPageAndRemovingTheFlagRestoresThePhoto`, `PublishOfficialPhotosTests.AWithheldPersonWithAnUnreadableNewPhotoDoesNotRestoreThePreviousPhoto`.
- A photo that the previous release holds under the same path is linked, and its capture is not read. A previous release that lacks the linked file fails the publish. Enforced by `ReleasePhotos` and `IReleaseStaging.LinkFileAsync`. Tests: `PublishOfficialPhotosTests.AnUnchangedPhotoIsLinkedFromThePreviousReleaseAndItsCaptureIsNotReadAgain`, `PublishOfficialPhotosTests.APreviousReleaseThatLacksTheLinkedFileFailsThePublishInsteadOfDroppingThePhoto`.
- `releases publish` needs `CIVIC_LENS_ISSUE_ICONS_CONFIG`. `releases publish` and the review workspace need `CIVIC_LENS_ARTIFACT_DIRECTORY`, and refuse to start without it with exit code 2. The review workspace checks the variable whenever `CIVIC_LENS_RELEASE_DIRECTORY` is set, so it stops even though a missing issues configuration only turns its release lists off. The worker stage always has its command line directory. Enforced by `PublicationComposition.RequireArtifactRoot` and `PublicationComposition.RequireIssueIcons`. Test: `ReleaseCommandTests.PublishingRefusesToStartWithoutTheArtifactDirectoryOrTheIssueIconFileBecauseANewPhotoNeedsItsCaptureAndATileNeedsItsIcon`. The workspace startup check has no test.
- A new photo whose capture is missing, corrupt, or not a JPEG of the published size logs an error. The profile keeps the previous photo, else it has no photo. Only `IOException`, `InvalidDataException`, and `NotSupportedException` take this path. Enforced by `ReleasePhotos`. Tests: `PublishOfficialPhotosTests.AnUnreadableNewPhotoKeepsThePhotoOfThePreviousReleaseAndLogsAnError`, `PublishOfficialPhotosTests.AnUnreadableNewPhotoWithNoPreviousPhotoGivesNoPhotoLogsAnErrorAndStillPublishes`.
- A change of the photo, or the loss of it, rewrites the page of the official, because the publish compares the whole profile. No extra carry rule exists. Enforced by `ReleaseOfficialPages`. Tests: `PublishOfficialPhotosTests.ANewPhotoRewritesTheOfficialPageBecauseTheProfileChanged`, `PublishOfficialPhotosTests.APhotoThatLeavesTheProfileRewritesTheOfficialPageAndWritesNoFile`.
- The current page shows the portrait in the header, beside the name. The header sits in the navy hero band of `PublicPage`. The portrait has the alt text "Official portrait of {name}" and the credit. An official with no photo gets a hidden placeholder in the same frame. The line "No portrait is shown for this official." also covers a withheld photo. On a narrow screen the portrait is above the name. A past Congress page shows no portrait. Enforced by `OfficialPortrait`, `PublishedOfficialPage`, and the `.official-hero` rules of `public.css`. No test checks the side by side layout. Checked in Chromium at 360 and 1280 px, in both themes. Tests: `PublishedOfficialPageTests.TheHeaderHoldsTheNameThePortraitAndTheLinksRowSoTheyReadAsOneIntroduction`, `PublishedOfficialPageTests.TheCurrentPageShowsThePortraitFromTheReleaseRootWithItsSizeAltTextAndCredit`, `PublishedOfficialPageTests.AnOfficialWithNoPhotoShowsAHiddenPlaceholderInTheSameFrameAndSaysSo`, `PublishedOfficialPageTests.APastCongressPageShowsNoPortraitBecauseOnlyTheCurrentPageHasOne`.
- The officials page lists every official of `officials.json` as a card. A card shows the portrait, the name linked to the official page, the office of the latest term, and the party. A term that ended before the release date reads "Former" before the office, and the card stays under its office tab. A caucus that differs from the party reads ", caucuses with" and the caucus. The list has `role="list"`. It shows the count as a sentence such as "12 records other than votes". An official with none shows "0 records other than votes". An executive card shows no state. Enforced by `PublishedOfficialsPage` and `PublishedOfficialView`. Tests: `PublishedOfficialPageTests.TheOfficialListShowsOneCardForEveryOfficialWithTheLinkOfficeAndPartyAndAFullSentenceCount`, `PublishedOfficialPageTests.AnExecutiveCardShowsTheOfficeWithNoStateBecauseAnExecutiveHasNone`, `PublishedOfficialPageTests.AnEndedLatestTermReadsFormerButStillGroupsUnderItsOfficeSoTheReaderKnowsTheOfficialLeft`, `PublishedOfficialPageTests.ATermEndsByTheReleaseDateNotTheRenderClockSoAListPublishedBeforeTheEndShowsTheOfficeAsCurrent`, `PublishedOfficialPageTests.ACaucusThatDiffersFromThePartyIsNamedSoAnIndependentShowsWhoseCaucusTheyJoin`, `PublishedOfficialPageTests.TheCardListKeepsItsListRoleBecauseListStyleNoneDropsItInSafari`.
- The latest term is the term with the latest start that is not after the release date. The official page and the officials page share `PublishedOfficialView.LatestTerm`. Enforced by `PublishedOfficialView`. Tests: `PublishedOfficialPageTests.TheLatestTermByStartDecidesTheOfficeAndKindSoASenatorWhoWasARepresentativeIsASenator`, `PublishedOfficialPageTests.ACardIgnoresATermThatStartsAfterTheReleaseDateSoTheListMatchesThePage`, `PublishedOfficialPageTests.ATermThatStartsAfterTheReleaseDateIsNotTheLatestTermSoTheLedeDescribesTheOfficeTheyHoldToday`.
- A term that ended before the release date reads "Former" on the card and in the lede of the official page. The lede then has a closed tenure. Enforced by `PublishedOfficialView.CardOffice`, `PublishedOfficialView.Lede`, and `PublishedOfficialView.Tenure`. Tests: `PublishedOfficialPageTests.AnEndedLatestTermReadsFormerButStillGroupsUnderItsOfficeSoTheReaderKnowsTheOfficialLeft`, `PublishedOfficialPageTests.AFormerMemberGetsAClosedTenureAndFormerBecauseSinceWouldBeFalse`.
- The offices table lists only terms that started by the release date. It has one row for each unbroken stretch of terms in the same office, state, district, and party. A gap of more than 7 days starts a new row, so a return after a loss or a resignation shows two rows. Enforced by `PublishedOfficialView.OfficeStretches`. Tests: `PublishedOfficialPageTests.TermsInTheSameSeatThatFollowEachOtherMakeOneRowEvenWhenTheNextOneStartsAfterTheLateSwearingInOfJanuary1997`, `PublishedOfficialPageTests.ATermThatStartsEightDaysAfterTheLastEndedStartsANewRowBecauseNoSwearingInIsThatLate`, `PublishedOfficialPageTests.AnOfficialWhoLeftOfficeAndCameBackInsideOneCongressGetsTwoRowsBecauseTheStretchWasBroken`, `PublishedOfficialPageTests.ATermWithADifferentStateDistrictPartyOrOfficeStartsANewRowBecauseTheRowNamesOneSeat`, `PublishedOfficialPageTests.AnOfficeThatStartsAfterTheReleaseDateIsNotInTheOfficesTableSoItIsNeverShownAsPresent`.
- The record list of the record page has a count line above it. The count line counts the published records by kind and the votes with their Congress. An empty list has no count line. Enforced by `PublishedOfficialView.RecordsCountLine`. Tests: `PublishedOfficialPageTests.TheRecordsCountLineCountsEachKindAndTheVotesWithTheirCongressSoNoCountHasAnUnstatedScope`, `PublishedOfficialPageTests.TheRecordsCountLineUsesTheSingularForOneRecordAndItsOwnKind`, `PublishedOfficialPageTests.AnEmptyRecordsTableHasNoCountLineBecauseItsEmptyStateAlreadySaysThereAreNoRecords`.
- Card portraits load lazily, and the profile portrait loads eagerly. A card with no photo shows the placeholder. Enforced by `OfficialPortrait`. Tests: `PublishedOfficialPageTests.CardPortraitsLoadLazilyAndACardWithNoPhotoShowsThePlaceholder`, `PublishedOfficialPageTests.TheProfilePortraitStaysEagerBecauseItIsTheFirstThingOnThePage`.
- The page shows one credit line below the list when every photo has the same credit. When credits differ, each card with a photo shows its own credit. No credit text is hidden. Enforced by `PublishedOfficialsPage`. Tests: `PublishedOfficialPageTests.TheCreditShowsOnceBelowTheListWhenEveryPhotoHasTheSameCredit`, `PublishedOfficialPageTests.TheCreditShowsOnEachCardWhenCreditsDifferSoNoLicenseTextIsHidden`.
- Each card carries `data-name`, `data-office`, and `data-state`. The tabs name who they show and give the count, for example "Senators (100)". The tabs are "Senators", "Representatives", "Executives", "Justices", and "Other officials", in that order. A card goes in the tab of the `Kind` of its latest term. A tab shows only when it has members, except "All officials". An official with no term is in "Other officials". So is an official whose terms all start after the release date. Enforced by `PublishedOfficialsPage`. Tests: `PublishedOfficialPageTests.TheCardsCarryTheDataTheBrowserFiltersOnAndTheTabsSayWhoAndHowMany`, `PublishedOfficialPageTests.AnOfficialWithNoTermIsInTheOtherOfficialsTabAndAllOfficialsOnly`, `PublishedOfficialPageTests.AnOfficialWhoseTermsAllStartAfterTheReleaseDateIsInTheOtherOfficialsTabBecauseNoTermIsTheLatest`, `PublishedOfficialPageTests.AJudicialOfficeIsInTheJusticesTabAfterExecutivesAndAnExecutiveOfficeIsInExecutivesNotOtherOfficials`.
- The filter controls are hidden in the static HTML, and `public.js` shows them. So a reader without JavaScript sees every card and no dead control. `public.js` filters by office, state, and name in the browser, and a status region says "Showing N of M officials". Enforced by `PublishedOfficialsPage` and `public.js`. Test for the markup: `PublishedOfficialPageTests.TheFilterControlsAreHiddenInTheStaticPageSoAReaderWithoutScriptSeesEveryCardAndNoDeadControl`. No test for the script, because the repository has no JavaScript test harness.
- `catalog.json` names every issue and policy area of the release, and an ID with no configured name names itself. Enforced by `ReleaseDataFiles` from `ReleaseFacets`. Test: `PublishOfficialsTests.TheCatalogNamesEveryIssueAndPolicyAreaOfTheReleaseAndAnUnconfiguredIdNamesItself`.
- `bill-status.json` is written before the manifest, holds only action records of the release, and is validated on write and on read. A row has an action record ID, a date, and a UTC read time. It has a trimmed text of 1 to 1,000 characters with no control character. A file over `PublishedBillStatuses.MaximumBytes` is refused on read and on write. The cap is the record limit times 4 KB. A row of the longest allowed text is under 4 KB. Real rows are about 1 KB or less. The longest text in 16,808 dev bill details has 652 characters. A release from before the file reads as having no status. Enforced by `ReleaseBillStatuses`, `PreviousRelease`, `PublishedBillStatus.Validate`, and `PublishedBillStatuses.Validate`. Tests: `ReleaseIngestTests.TheBillStatusFileOfAReleaseIsNeverFetchedSoAStatusLineCostsTheSearchIndexNothing`, `PublishBillStatusTests.AnActionWithAStoredStatusPublishesItsRowAndRendersTheStatusButAnActionWithNoneGetsNeitherSoNoReaderSeesAFalseNoActionLine`, `PublishBillStatusTests.AReleaseFromBeforeTheStatusFileReadsAsHavingNoStatusSoTheFirstPublishWritesEveryPageThatNowHasOne`, `PublishBillStatusTests.APreviousStatusFileOverTheByteCapIsRefusedOnReadSoNoPublishBuildsOnAnUnboundedFile`, `PublishedBillStatusesTests.ARowThatBreaksTheRulesIsRefusedSoNoReaderShowsAHalfTrueStatus`, `PublishedBillStatusesTests.ARowOfTheLongestAllowedTextStaysUnderTheByteCapOfARowEvenWithTheWidestCharacters`, `PublishedBillStatusesTests.ADuplicateRecordIsRefusedBecauseOneActionHasOneStatus`, `PublishedBillStatusesTests.ARowWithNoDateIsRefusedOnReadSoNoReaderGuessesADefault`, and `PublishedBillStatusesTests.AFileOfTheMaximumNumberOfRealisticRowsStaysUnderTheByteCapSoTheCapNeverRefusesARealRelease`.
- The status of an action is the latest action of the newest stored version of its bill. A newer version with no latest action gives no status, so an older status never shows once the bill moved. Enforced by `PostgresActionReviewStore.ReadBillStatusesAsync`. Test: `PostgresBillStreamTests.TheStatusOfAnActionIsTheLatestActionOfTheNewestVersionOfItsBillSoAnOlderStatusNeverShowsOnceTheBillMoved`.
- A withdrawn record has no status row, and a returned record gets its row again. Enforced by `ReleaseBillStatuses` and `PublishRelease`. Tests: `PublishBillStatusTests.AWithdrawnActionAndACarriedEarlierWithdrawalHaveNoRowButAReturnedActionGetsItsRowAgain`.
- A carried action page is written again only when its latest action text or date differs from the previous release. A row that appears or disappears counts as a difference. A status keeps the read time of the release that first showed its text and date. The file and the page then never show two times. The page keeps its Related panel. A carried vote page keeps its Related panel too, when its bill action is withdrawn or returned. A publish with no record and no other change still publishes when a status changed. Enforced by `ReleaseBillStatuses`, `ReleaseLinks`, and `ReleasePages`. Tests: `PublishBillStatusTests.ACarriedActionPageIsWrittenAgainOnlyWhenTheLatestActionOrItsDateChangesSoACheckAloneRewritesNoPageAndTheFileKeepsTheTimeThePageShows`, `PublishBillStatusTests.ARemovedStatusRowRendersTheCarriedPageAgainWithNoStatusLine`, `PublishBillStatusTests.AStatusRewriteKeepsTheRelatedPanelOfTheCarriedActionPageSoNoRelationshipLinkDisappears`, `PublishWithdrawalsTests.ACarriedVotePageWrittenAgainBecauseItsBillActionWasWithdrawnStillListsItsRelatedRecords`, and `PublishBillStatusTests.APublishThatOnlyRefreshesAStatusStillPublishesButOneWithNothingChangedIsRefused`.
- The action page shows the plain meaning of the latest action and its action date. It also shows the time that Civic Lens read it and a link to the bill. `BillStatusMeaning` is the one owner of the meaning. Each rule matches an anchored start of the text, in order. No rule matches a word inside the text. Other text shows as "Latest official action: " and the raw text. Nothing is hidden or guessed. No meaning calls a bill a success or a failure. The file keeps the raw text. Enforced by `BillStatusMeaning` and `PublishedActionView`. Tests: `BillStatusMeaningTests`, `ReleaseRendererTests.ActionPageStatesTheLatestActionWithItsDateAndReadTimeAndLinksTheBillOnCongressGovSoTheReaderCanCheckIt`, `ReleaseRendererTests.ActionPageShowsThePlainMeaningOfARecognizedLatestActionWithItsDateAndNotTheRawText`, and `ReleaseRendererTests.ActionPageWithNoStoredStatusShowsNoStatusLineSoItNeverClaimsThatNothingHappened`.
- The rows of the current official page and of its record page show a status line under each bill that the official sponsors. The line reads "Latest official action on <action date> (read from Congress.gov on <read date>): <plain meaning>". The action date is the date of the latest action. The read date is the time of the first read of that text and date, as on the action page. A page gets the line when a publish writes it. `BillStatusMeaning.Plain` gives the meaning. For a text with no rule it gives the official text as one sentence, so the line never says "Latest official action" twice. A bill with no stored status has no line. A cosponsor page has no line, because an action entry names only its sponsors. A changed status text or date also writes again the current page of each sponsor, because `ReleaseOfficialPages` treats those officials as touched. A status check that changes neither text nor date writes no official page. Enforced by `ReleaseOfficialPages`, `PublishedOfficialView.StatusLine`, and `PublishedOfficialRow`. Tests: `PublishOfficialPagesTests.AChangedBillStatusRewritesTheCurrentPageOfTheSponsorOnlySoTheLineNeverShowsAnOldStatus`, `PublishOfficialPagesTests.AStatusCheckThatChangesNeitherTextNorDateLeavesTheSponsorPageLinked`, `PublishOfficialPagesTests.AChangedBillStatusRewritesThePageOfEverySponsorOfTheBill`, `PublishOfficialPagesTests.ARewrittenSponsorPageKeepsTheFirstReadTimeOfAnUnchangedBillSoOnlyTheMovedBillShowsANewTime`, `PublishedOfficialPageTests.ASponsoredBillRowShowsTheActionDateTheReadDateAndThePlainMeaningOfItsLatestAction`, `PublishedOfficialPageTests.AReceivedActionNamesTheChamberThatPassedTheBillFromTheBillTypeOfTheRowSoTheLineSaysWhereTheBillIs`, `PublishedOfficialPageTests.ABillWithNoKnownMeaningShowsTheOfficialTextOnceAfterTheLeadSoTheLeadIsNotSaidTwice`, and `PublishedOfficialPageTests.ABillWithNoStoredStatusAndOtherRecordsShowNoStatusLine`.
- The worker publishes a release when a bill status changed and no record is ready. The tick checks the change first with `PublishRelease.HasBillStatusChangeAsync`, which stages nothing. It then publishes once with a status-only request whose key names the release it builds on. Enforced by `PublishApprovedRecords`. Tests: `PublishApprovedRecordsTests.ATickWithNoReadyRecordPublishesOneReleaseWhenABillStatusChangedAndThenStopsSoAMovedBillReachesTheSite` and `PublishApprovedRecordsTests.ATickWithNoReadyRecordAndNoStatusChangeMakesNoReleaseAndStagesNothing`, and `PublishApprovedRecordsTests.TheStatusOnlyFlagStaysOutOfTheRequestHashWhenItIsOffSoTheKeyOfAnEarlierRequestStillReplays`. The flag stays out of the request hash when it is off, so the key of an earlier request still replays.
- `officials.json` and `catalog.json` are written before the manifest, so an incomplete directory never looks complete. Enforced by `PublishRelease.StageAsync`. Tests: `PublishReleaseTests.ManifestIsWrittenLastSoAnIncompleteDirectoryNeverLooksComplete` and `PublishReleaseTests.EveryIndexPageIsWrittenBeforeTheManifestSoALongReleaseIsNeverTruncated`.
- A manifest of any other schema version is refused, and the version 1 files round trip and stay within their caps. The caps keep the file size bounded. The search service reads neither file yet. A publish reads `officials.json` of the previous release under `PublishedOfficials.MaximumBytes`, and refuses a file over the cap. It refuses to write a file over the cap. `catalog.json` is never read back. Enforced by `PublicationRelease.Validate`, `PublishedOfficials.Validate`, `PublishedCatalog.Validate`, `PreviousRelease`, and `ReleaseDataFiles`. Tests: `PublishOfficialPagesTests.APreviousOfficialsFileOverTheByteCapIsRefusedOnRead`, `PublishOfficialPagesTests.AnOfficialsFileOverTheByteCapIsRefusedBeforeItIsWritten`, `PublicationReleaseTests.RejectsAnyOtherSchemaVersion`, `PublishedOfficialsTests.AReleaseKeepsItsOfficialsAndCatalogThroughAJsonRoundTrip`, `PublishedOfficialsTests.TheFilesHaveSizeCapsSoTheirSizeStaysBounded`.
- A manifest entry requires `kind`, `officials`, `issueIds`, and `policyAreaIds`, and a manifest of another schema version is refused. An `officials.json` profile requires `terms`. No reader guesses a default for old data. One exception exists: `articleUrl` of a statement is optional, an old file omits it, and it then reads as null. No other field has a default. Enforced by `required` and `RespectNullableAnnotations` in `PublicationProtocol.JsonOptions`, and by `PublicationRelease.Validate`. Tests: `PublicationReleaseTests.AnEntryWithoutAFilterFieldIsRefusedSoNoReaderGuessesADefault`, `PublishedOfficialsTests.AProfileWithoutTermsIsRefusedSoAPreviousReleaseIsNeverReadAsHavingNoTerms`, `PublicationReleaseTests.AcceptsTheCurrentSchemaVersion`, `PublishStatementTests.AReleaseOfAnotherSchemaVersionCannotBeTheBaseOfTheNextRelease`, `PublishedStatementTests.ARecordWrittenBeforeTheArticleLinkExistedStillReadsWithNoLink`.
- The search service does not fetch the two new files. Enforced by `ReleaseFeed`, which fetches the manifest and the record files only. Test: `ReleaseIngestTests.TheOfficialsAndCatalogFilesOfAReleaseAreNeverFetchedSoTheSearchIndexHoldsTheSameRecords`.
- A publish rebuilds the files of an official only when the official is touched, and links every other file from the previous release. Three cases touch an official. An added or replaced manifest entry of any kind names the official, in the new revision or in the replaced one. A link that cites the entry can also name the official as speaker. An added or replaced vote names the official as a member, in the new revision or in the replaced one. The `officials.json` profile of the official differs from the previous release. Enforced by `ReleaseOfficialPages`. Tests: `PublishOfficialPagesTests.APublishRewritesOnlyThePagesOfTheOfficialsItTouchesAndLinksEveryOtherPage`, `PublishOfficialPagesTests.APublishThatNamesNoOfficialRewritesNoOfficialFile`, `PublishOfficialPagesTests.AReplacedRecordTouchesTheOfficialsOfItsOldRevisionToo`, `PublishOfficialPagesTests.ARosterChangeRewritesTheCurrentPageButLinksTheVoteFileAndTheEarlierCongressPage`.
- A replaced vote revision replaces its row, so a vote never appears twice. An official who left the member list loses the row, and an official with no row has no file. Enforced by `ReleaseOfficialVotes` and `PublishedOfficialVotes.Validate`. Tests: `PublishOfficialVotesTests.AReplacedVoteRevisionReplacesItsRowInsteadOfAddingADuplicate`, `PublishOfficialVotesTests.AnOfficialWhoLeftTheMemberListLosesTheRowEvenWhenAnotherRecordKeepsTheOfficialListed`, `PublishOfficialVotesTests.APublishThatReplacesTwoVotesOfDifferentCongressesRewritesTheFileOfEachCongress`, `PublishOfficialVotesTests.AnOfficialWithNoVoteGetsNoVoteFile`.
- A roll call keeps its chamber and its Congress across the revisions of its vote record. The official vote file orders its rows by day, chamber, session, and roll number. A replaced row is therefore always in the file of the new revision. The record ID is a hash of the chamber, the source, and the roll call, and a person revision cannot change the roll call. Enforced by `PostgresCollectionJobStore.HashVoteRecord` and `VoteDraftRevision.HasSameStructuredPartAs`. No test of the publish step. `PublishOfficialVotesTests.AVoteFileHoldingAVoteOfAnotherCongressIsRefusedSoAMisplacedFileCannotHideARow` covers the read side.
- A vote file is read back under `PublishedOfficialVotes.MaximumBytes`, and a full Congress of maximum length rows stays under that cap. A folder of an official lists at most `PublicationProtocol.MaximumOfficialFiles` files. Enforced by `ReleaseOfficialVotes`, `IReleaseDirectory.ReadFileAsync`, and `IReleaseDirectory.ListFilesAsync`. Tests: `PublishOfficialVotesTests.AVoteFileOverTheByteCapIsRefusedOnReadSoAHugeFileCannotExhaustMemory`, `PublishedOfficialVotesTests.AFullCongressOfMaximumLengthRowsStaysUnderTheByteCapSoTheFileCanAlwaysBeReadBack`, `FileReleaseDirectoryTests.ListingAFolderWithMoreFilesThanTheCapIsRefusedSoAFolderCannotGrowWithoutBound`.
- Every official that a published record names has `officials.json` and page entries. The record can name the official in its manifest entry, or as a member of a vote. The name comes from the configuration, else from the name that the published records hold. A person who left the configuration keeps the page, with that name and no terms. The page says that Civic Lens no longer tracks the person. A publish fails only when no record holds a name. Enforced by `PublicationOfficials`, `ReleaseDataFiles`, and `ReleaseOfficialPages`. Tests: `PublishOfficialPagesTests.AnOfficialWhoLeftTheConfigurationKeepsAPageWithTheNameOfTheVoteRecordAndNoTerms`, `PublishOfficialPagesTests.AnOfficialWhoLeftTheConfigurationAndIsNamedByAnEntryTakesTheNameOfTheEntryWithoutReadingAVoteRecord`, `PublishOfficialPagesTests.APublishWithNoEarlierProfileStillNamesAnOfficialWhoLeftTheConfigurationFromTheRecords`, `PublishOfficialPagesTests.APublishFailsLoudlyWhenNoRecordHoldsTheNameOfAnOfficialWhoLeftTheConfiguration`, `PublishedOfficialPageTests.AnOfficialWhoLeftTheConfigurationSaysThatCivicLensNoLongerTracksThem`, `PublishOfficialPagesTests.AnOfficialWithNoPublishedRecordGetsNoPageSoNoEmptyProfileExists`.
- A record page links an official because every named official has a page. An action page links the sponsor and not a cosponsor, because only the sponsor owns the action. Enforced by `PublishedRecordView.OfficialLink` and `PublishedOfficialName`. Tests: `ReleaseRendererTests.RecordPagesLinkEachSpeakerToTheOfficialPageBecauseEverySpeakerOfAPublishedRecordHasOne`, `ReleaseRendererTests.ActionPageLinksTheSponsorButNotACosponsorBecauseOnlyTheSponsorOwnsTheAction`, `ReleaseRendererTests.VotePageLinksEachTrackedMemberToTheOfficialPage`, `PublishedReleaseOutlivesDatabaseTests.PublishedReleaseServesFromFilesAloneAfterTheDatabaseIsGone`.
- The current Congress of an official is the newest Congress with a vote file of that official. Enforced by `ReleaseOfficialPages`. The current page lists the entries that name the official and the votes of that Congress. Each earlier Congress has its own page of votes. Every page of an official with two or more Congresses shows tabs below the lede, one for each Congress, newest first. The first tab links to the current page and the tab of the page itself is marked current. The first tab reads "All records, votes of the 119th Congress (2025 to 2027)". The label comes from the Congress number only. No date in it can go stale, so a page carried by hard link stays true. Each earlier tab reads "118th Congress (votes only)". A publish rewrites the earlier pages of an official when the list of Congresses changes. Enforced by `ReleaseOfficialPages`, `PublishedOfficialView.CongressTabs`, and `PublishedOfficialPage.razor`. Tests: `PublishedOfficialPageTests.TheCurrentPageTabsLinkEachEarlierCongressFromItsOwnFolderAndSayThoseCongressesHoldVotesOnly`, `PublishedOfficialPageTests.AnEarlierCongressPageKeepsTheTabsWithALinkBackToTheCurrentPageAndMarksItsOwnTab`, `PublishedOfficialPageTests.TheFirstTabNamesBothScopesAndTheYearsOfItsCongressWhateverTheCongress`, `PublishedOfficialPageTests.TheTabLabelsDoNotDependOnThePublishDateSoACarriedPageStaysTrue`, `PublishedOfficialPageTests.AnOfficialWithOneCongressGetsNoTabsBecauseThereIsNothingToSwitchTo`, `PublishOfficialPagesTests.ANewVoteOfTheCurrentCongressLeavesTheEarlierCongressFilesLinkedAndANewerCongressRewritesEveryEarlierPageBecauseTheirTabsListEveryCongress`, `PublishOfficialPagesTests.TheCurrentCongressIsTheNewestWithAVoteAndEarlierCongressesGetOnePageEach`, `PublishOfficialPagesTests.WhenTheNewestCongressEmptiesTheNextOlderCongressBecomesCurrentAndItsPastPageIsDropped`, `PublishOfficialPagesTests.WithdrawingTheOnlyVoteOfAMiddleCongressRewritesTheOtherEarlierPageAndDropsThePageOfTheEmptiedCongress`, `PublishOfficialPagesTests.ARenamedOfficialRewritesTheEarlierCongressPagesBecauseTheyShowTheName`.
- `releases publish --rewrite-officials` writes the current page and every past page of every official, and links none of them. The option itself adds no record. It keeps the release history, the withdrawals, and the first publication date of each record. Enforced by `ReleaseOfficialPages` and `PublishRelease`. Tests: `PublishOfficialPagesTests.ARewriteOfAllOfficialsWritesTheCurrentAndPastPagesOfEveryOfficialAndLinksNone`, `PublishOfficialPagesTests.WithoutTheRewriteOptionAnUntouchedOfficialKeepsItsCurrentAndPastPagesByLink`, `PublishOfficialPagesTests.ARewriteOfAllOfficialsKeepsEveryRecordFirstPublicationDateAndEveryWithdrawal`.
- A vote manifest entry may hold `yeaCount`, `nayCount`, `alignment`, and `chamber`. An action manifest entry may hold `cosponsorIds`. All five are optional, and a missing field means no data. A reader never guesses a default. The schema version stays 1, so `PublicationProtocol.JsonOptions` still reads a manifest from before these fields. `yeaCount` and `nayCount` come together from the whole chamber totals. `alignment` is `split`, `partiesAgree`, or `mixed`. `split` means the party majorities took opposite sides. `partiesAgree` means both majorities took the same side. `mixed` means both major parties voted, but one has no majority, as in a tie. A vote with no Yea and Nay totals has none of the three. A vote of a kind that is not substantive has no `alignment`. A vote that lacks a Democrat row or a Republican row has no `alignment`. `chamber` is `house` or `senate`. `PublishedVote.ToEntry` copies it from the vote record, which `PublishedVoteBuilder` fills from the revision, so every vote entry that a new build writes holds it. `PublishedRecordEntry.Validate` accepts a vote entry without it, because a release from before the field has none. A release from before the field keeps its entries without `chamber` until `--rewrite-entries` runs. No other kind may hold it. The entry rows hold no `chamber` column, as they hold no vote count. `cosponsorIds` is an empty list when the action has no current cosponsor. It lists the official ID of each cosponsor once. It skips a withdrawn cosponsorship. It skips an official who also sponsors the same action. It holds at most `PublishedAction.MaximumSponsorships` IDs. The entry field `officials` still names the sponsors only, so a cosponsor never counts as a sponsored bill and gets no page. Enforced by `PublishedRecordEntry.Validate`, `PublishedVote.ToEntry`, and `PublishedAction.ToEntry`. Tests: `PublicationReleaseTests.OnlyAVoteEntryMayNameAChamberAndItNamesAKnownOneSoNoOtherKindCarriesAFalseChamber`, `PublicationReleaseTests.AVoteEntryWithNoChamberStaysValidSoAReleaseFromBeforeTheFieldStillReads`, `PublishVoteTests.TheManifestEntryNamesTheChamberOfTheVoteRevisionSoAPageCanListOneChamberWithoutReadingTheVoteFile`, `PublicationReleaseTests.AnEntryFromBeforeTheVoteAndCosponsorFieldsStillReadsAndKeepsThemEmptySoNoReaderGuessesAValue`, `PublicationReleaseTests.VoteFactsBelongToAVoteEntryAsAPairWithBoundedCountsSoNoOtherKindCarriesThem`, `PublicationReleaseTests.CosponsorsBelongToAnActionEntryAndAreDistinctAndBoundedSoOneBillCannotSwellTheManifest`, `PublishedVoteTests.TheEntryNamesHowTheTwoPartiesVotedFromTheirMajorities`, `PublishedVoteTests.AVoteWithNoMajorPartyRowHasNoAlignmentBecauseNoReaderCanCompareTwoPartiesWhenOneIsAbsent`, `PublishedActionTests.TheEntryListsTheCosponsorIdsApartFromTheSponsorsSoACosponsorNeverCountsAsASponsoredBill`, `PublishedActionTests.AWithdrawnCosponsorshipIsNotListedBecauseTheOfficialNoLongerBacksTheBill`, `PublishedActionTests.ASponsorWhoAlsoCosponsorsIsListedAsASponsorOnlySoTheBillNeverCountsTwiceForOneOfficial`, `PublishedActionTests.TwoCosponsorRowsOfOneOfficialGiveOneIdSoTheEntryStaysValidAndThePublishGoesOn`, `PublicationReleaseTests.AManifestOfTheMaximumNumberOfRealisticActionEntriesWithCosponsorsStaysUnderTheManifestByteCap`, `PublishEntryRewriteTests.AReleaseFromBeforeTheNewFieldsStillReadsAndAPlainPublishKeepsEveryCarriedEntryAsItWas`.
- An action manifest entry may hold `billLine`. It is one line of 1 to 200 characters that says what the bill does. `BillLine` builds it from the summary citation of the action record. That citation quotes the summary of the Congressional Research Service (CRS). The text starts after the `Text:` line, which `CongressBillRenderer.SummaryTextLabel` owns. `BillLine` reads the lines of the text in order and skips empty lines. A line that ends with `.`, `?`, or `!` gives the sentence. A closing `"`, `”`, `’`, or `)` may follow the final mark. A line with no final mark is a title line. It has at most 200 characters and ends with a letter, a digit, or `)`. `BillLine` skips a title line. A line that ends with `:` is the start of a list, so the action has no line. Any other line with no final mark gives no line. A sentence ends at `.`, `?`, or `!` in two cases only. The mark, with its closing characters, ends the line. Or white space follows them. Then comes a capital letter, `"`, `“`, or `(`. A mark does not end a sentence after a token of single letters with periods, such as `U.S.`, `D.C.`, `U.S.C.`, or `H.R.`. It does not end a sentence after `St.`, `Mr.`, `Mrs.`, `Dr.`, `No.`, `Sec.`, or `Pub.`. `BillLine.Abbreviations` lists these. A sentence over 200 characters is cut at the last white space, a non-breaking space too. The line and the cut marker `…` stay within 200 characters. The kept sentence includes the closing characters. The cut drops a trailing comma, semicolon, or colon. A sentence with no white space inside the cap gives no line. An action with no summary citation has no line. The line never comes from the headline, the summary of the record, or the code-written text. No other kind may hold it. The record file owns the line, as it owns `chamber`. No entry column holds it, and `ReleaseSplice` takes it from the file. `--rewrite-entries` fills the line of an older action entry from its record file. Enforced by `BillLine`, `PublishedRecordEntry.Validate`, `ReleaseSplice`, and `RecordFileEntry`. Tests: `PublishActionTests.TheEntryLineIsTheFirstSentenceOfTheCrsSummaryBecauseOneRowNeedsOneShortLine`, `PublishActionTests.ASummaryWithNoSentenceOrAListIntroHasNoLineBecauseAListItemIsNotTheSummary`, `PublishActionTests.ALongFirstSentenceIsCutAtAWordBoundaryAndEndsWithTheCutMarkerWithinTheCap`, `PublishActionTests.ACutDropsATrailingCommaSemicolonOrColonBeforeTheCutMarker`, `PublishActionTests.ANonBreakingSpaceIsAWordBoundaryForTheCut`, `PublishActionTests.ASentenceWithNoWhitespaceInsideTheCapHasNoLine`, `PublishActionTests.AnActionWithNoCrsSummaryHasNoLineAndNeverShowsTheActionTextInItsPlace`, `PublicationReleaseTests.AnActionEntryWithoutABillLineStillReadsAndKeepsItEmptySoNoReaderGuessesALine`, `PublicationReleaseTests.ABillLineRoundTripsAndFillsTheCapExactly`, `PublicationReleaseTests.ABillLineBelongsToAnActionEntryAndIsBoundedSoNoOtherKindCarriesOne`, `PublishEntryRewriteTests.TheRewriteOptionFillsTheBillLineOfAnOldActionEntryBecauseTheRecordFileOwnsTheLine`, and `PublishEntryRewriteTests.TheRewriteOptionKeepsTheSameBillLineOfACarriedEntryThatHoldsOne`.
- `PublishedBillLine` shows one bill line and no source mark. Each table or panel that shows a line says the source once, with the text "From the Congressional Research Service summary." (`PublishedRecordLabels.BillLineSource`). The record table adds it to its notes through `PublishedVoteView.ListNotes`. The official record rows, the home latest votes, the closest vote card, and the bill panel of a vote page show it through `PublishedBillLineNote`. A table with no line shows no note. A sorted table in the browser adds the note when the sorted rows hold a line and the page has none. Enforced by `PublishedRecordTable`, `PublishedRecordRows`, `PublishedHomeLatest`, `PublishedBoardPage`, `PublishedVotePage`, and `public.js`. Tests: `ReleaseRendererTests.AllRecordsPageShowsTheBillLineOfEachActionAndOfAVoteOnTheirBillsAndTheSourceNoteOnceSoNoRowRepeatsIt`, `ReleaseRendererTests.APageWithNoBillLineShowsNoSourceNoteBecauseTheNoteBelongsOnlyToTheLinesThatItNames`, `ReleaseRendererTests.AnIssuePageShowsTheBillLineOfItsVoteWithTheSourceNoteOnceBecauseItUsesTheSameTableAsAllRecords`, `ReleaseRendererTests.TheVotePageShowsTheBillLineOfTheLinkedActionWithTheSourceNoteOnceAndNoneWhenTheActionHoldsNoLine`, `PublishedOfficialPageTests.AnActionRowShowsTheBillLineAndTheTableSaysItsSourceOnceWhileOtherRowsShowNone`, `PublishedOfficialPageTests.AnOfficialRecordPageWithNoBillLineShowsNoSourceNote`, `PublishedHomePageTests.TheLatestVotesShowTheBillLineOfTheirActionWithTheSourceNoteOnceAndShowNoNoteWhenNoVoteHoldsALine`, and `PublishedBoardPageTests.TheClosestVoteCardShowsTheBillLineOfItsVoteWithTheSourceNoteAndNoneWhenTheVoteHoldsNoLine`. The sort script has no test.
- A vote shows the bill line of the first action on its bill that the release holds. That is also the action that its page links. The vote entry holds no bill, so `ReleaseBillLines` maps each vote to that action and its line. `PublishRelease` builds the map once for every publish. It uses the entries of the new release and one batch read of the bill actions of all release votes. The map goes to the vote pages, the all records pages, the issue and policy area pages, the home page, and the closest vote card. Official pages show no line on vote rows. Enforced by `ReleaseBillLines.From`, `PublicationBuild.WithVoteBills`, and `PublishRelease`. Tests: `ReleaseBillLinesTests.AVoteTakesTheLineOfTheFirstActionOnItsBillThatTheReleaseHoldsBecauseThatIsTheActionItsPageLinks`, `ReleaseBillLinesTests.AVoteWhoseActionIsNotInTheReleaseHasNoBillAndAVoteWhoseActionHoldsNoLineLinksTheActionWithNoLine`, `PublishVoteTests.TheVotePageAndTheListPagesShowTheBillLineOfTheActionOnItsBillBecauseOneMapOfTheBuildFeedsEveryPage`, `PublishVoteTests.TheHomeHighlightsGetTheSameBillLinesAsTheListPages`, and `PublishPageRewriteTests.EveryPublishAsksTheDatabaseForTheBillActionsOfAllReleaseVotesOnceBecauseOneQueryForEachVoteWouldCostTooMuch`.
- A carried vote page renders again when an action on its bill is withdrawn, returns, or gets another bill line in this publish. Enforced by `ReleasePages.CarriedWithChangedFacts`. Tests: `PublishVoteTests.ACarriedVotePageRendersAgainWhenAnActionWithABillLineJoinsItsBillAndStaysAsItWasWhenAnUnrelatedRecordPublishes` and `PublishVoteTests.ACarriedVotePageStaysAsItWasWhenANewRevisionOfItsActionKeepsTheSameBillLine`.
- A plain publish copies the carried entries as they are. `releases publish --rewrite-entries` reads the record file of each carried vote and action, builds its entry again with `ToEntry`, and replaces the entry. An action entry gets its `billLine` from `BillLine.EntryOf`. It stops with `ReleaseRewriteException` that names the record kind and ID, and `ReleaseCommand` prints the message and exits with 1. This happens when a record file is invalid. It also happens when the old entry and the rebuilt entry differ in a field other than the six new fields. It changes no other entry field, links every record file from the previous release, and does not bring back a withdrawn record. It needs an active release and counts as a change, so it can publish with no record. The option is in the payload hash. Enforced by `ReleaseEntryRewrite`, `ReleaseCommand`, and `PublishRelease`. Tests: `PublishEntryRewriteTests.TheRewriteNamesTheRecordWhenItsFileIsNotAValidRecordSoTheOperatorKnowsWhichFileToCheck`, `ReleaseCommandTests.ARewriteThatFindsARecordFileOutOfStepWithItsEntryPrintsTheKindAndIdAndExitsWithOneSoTheOperatorKnowsWhichFileToCheck`, `PublishEntryRewriteTests.TheRewriteStopsOnACarriedEntryThatDiffersFromItsRecordFileOutsideTheNewFieldsSoItNeverHidesACorruptRelease`, `PublishEntryRewriteTests.TheRewriteOfACorrectedRecordKeepsTheRevisionOfTheEntryBecauseTheRecordFileIsTheCorrectedOne`, `ReleaseCommandTests.TheRewriteEntriesAndRewriteOfficialsOptionsRunTogetherAsInTheRunbookAndFillEntriesAndSummaries`, `PublishEntryRewriteTests.TheRewriteOptionFillsTheNewFieldsFromTheRecordFilesAndLeavesEveryOtherFieldAndEveryRecordFileAsItWas`, `PublishEntryRewriteTests.TheRewriteOptionWithNoChangeToAnyRecordIsStillAPublishSoTheOperatorCanRunItAlone`, `PublishEntryRewriteTests.TheRewriteOptionDoesNotBringBackAWithdrawnRecordBecauseOnlyCarriedEntriesAreRebuilt`, `PublishEntryRewriteTests.TheRewriteOptionIsInThePayloadHashSoTheSameKeyIsAMismatchWithoutIt`, `ReleaseCommandTests.TheRewriteEntriesOptionPublishesWithNoRecordAndFillsTheVoteFactsOfACarriedVote`.
- `officials-summary.json` holds one row for each official of `officials.json`. A row holds the Congress of the newest vote file of the official. It holds the split votes cast and the breaks for each chamber and party. It holds the roll calls held and missed for each chamber. It also holds `cosponsoredBills`, the count of action records that list the official as a current cosponsor. `cosponsoredBills` is null while any action entry of the release has no `cosponsorIds`, so a partial count never reads as a true count. A release with complete data holds a real number, and a real 0 is a count. The vote figures come from the same `TalkAndActionProfile` as the official page. A touched official gets new figures. An untouched official keeps the row of the previous release. A row with no Congress holds no vote data, and a reader must not read it as zero. An official with two or more vote Congresses also has `previous` in the row. A touched official reuses the carried `previous` when its Congress file is unchanged, and `--rewrite-officials` always computes it again. It holds the Congress, the split votes, and the roll calls of the next older Congress with a vote file. `previous` is optional, and a missing `previous` means no data. It holds a Congress older than the row Congress. A board needs it to keep an earlier Congress. Without it, a board shows the newest Congress. A row from before this field has no `previous` until `releases publish <key> --rewrite-officials` runs once. Enforced by `PublishedOfficialSummary.Validate`, `PublishedVoteFigures`, and `ReleaseOfficialSummaries`. Tests: `PublishOfficialPagesTests.ATouchedOfficialReusesTheCarriedPreviousFiguresWhenTheOlderCongressFileIsUnchangedAndComputesThemAgainWhenTheCarriedRowIsOutOfStep`, `PublishOfficialPagesTests.ANewVoteInTheOlderCongressChangesThePreviousFiguresAndAReplacementThatRemovesOneOfHerVotesLowersThemSoACarriedRowNeverGoesStale`, `PublishOfficialPagesTests.ARewriteOfAllOfficialsComputesThePreviousFiguresAgainSoItCorrectsACarriedRow`, and `PublishOfficialPagesTests.TheSummaryRowOfAnOfficialWithTwoCongressesHoldsThePreviousCongressSoABoardCanKeepShowingItAndAOneCongressRowHoldsNone`. `--rewrite-officials` touches every official. The cosponsor count comes from the manifest entries of each publish, so it never goes stale. A cosponsor who has no other record has no official file and no row. The search service never reads the file. Enforced by `ReleaseOfficialSummaries`, `ReleaseOfficialPages`, `PublishedOfficialSummaries.Validate`, and `RecordReader`, which reads manifest entries and record files only. Tests: `PublishEntryRewriteTests.TheCosponsorCountIsNullWhileAnActionEntryHasNoCosponsorDataAndARealCountIncludingZeroAfterTheRewrite`, `PublishEntryRewriteTests.AnOldActionEntryAndANewActionEntryTogetherGiveNoCountToAnyOfficialBecauseOnePartialEntryMakesEveryCountPartial`, `PublishEntryRewriteTests.AReleaseWithNoActionCountsZeroCosponsoredBillsBecauseNoActionEntryLacksData`, `PublishEntryRewriteTests.ACosponsorCountsInTheSummaryOfAnOfficialWhoIsNotTouchedAndNeverAsASponsoredBill`, `PublishEntryRewriteTests.AnUntouchedOfficialKeepsTheVoteFiguresOfThePreviousSummaryAndATouchedOfficialGetsNewOnes`, `PublishEntryRewriteTests.AReleaseWithNoSummaryFileGivesAnUntouchedOfficialNoVoteDataUntilARewriteOfAllOfficialsBecauseNoReaderGuessesADefault`, `PublishedOfficialSummariesTests.ARowIsBoundedAndConsistentSoARankingNeverReadsANumberThatCannotBeTrue`, `ReleaseIngestTests.TheOfficialSummariesFileOfAReleaseIsNeverFetchedSoARankingFileCostsTheSearchIndexNothing`.
- A board page shows the figures of one Congress for its chamber. Both parties of the chamber show the same Congress. That Congress is the newest Congress of the chamber once any member in it has at least `OfficialBoardPage.MinimumSplitVotes` (25) split votes cast. Until then the board keeps showing the next older Congress that any row holds, as its current figures or in `previous`. So the lists are not empty. With no older figures in the chamber, the board shows the newest Congress. A row with only attendance in the newest Congress counts as a row of that Congress, but it never counts toward the 25. A House at one Congress never hides a Senate at another. The page lede names the Congress.
